# Benchmarks library (RMBC II)
> Every numeric threshold, benchmark, price, rate, length and economics figure in the 58 RMBC II lesson pages, in topic tables with who said it and the lesson timestamp. Conflicting values sit side by side in [§ Conflicting values](#conflicting-values-side-by-side); numbers that differ from Evolve Start Here's KPI library are in [§ Evolve comparison](#evolve-start-here-comparison).

## 🤖 Agent use
- **Use when:** you need a number to plan or judge an RMBC asset or funnel: VSL length, section size, close share, engagement checkpoint, conversion baseline, landing-page CTR, funnel score band, CPA/AOV/COGS math, upsell length and take rate, email timing and discount ladder, test duration, webinar KPI.
- **Inputs → outputs:** the asset or decision → the [quick reference](#decision-quick-reference-derived) row → the full topic table → a number with its source and its claim label. If two rows disagree, use the conflict table's agent default.
- **Rules:**
  - **DO** grep by metric or lesson: `grep -n "take rate" benchmarks.md`, `grep -n "\[16-05" benchmarks.md`, `grep -n "^| B1" benchmarks.md`.
  - **DO** quote the claim label with the number: almost every result here is "(instructor claim)" or "(guest claim)" from one account, deck or anecdote, not a controlled study. Patrick Stiles: "these aren't split tests". [16-05 11:23–11:52](../lessons/16-05-vsl-benchmarks-data-and-best-practices-with-patrick-stiles.md)
  - **DO** prefer your own account data and an operator's benchmark for your niche over any figure here ("is that conversion rate good? Is the CPA high, low, average?"). [16-03 32:18–33:15](../lessons/16-03-funnel-economics-with-spg.md)
  - **DON'T** reuse any row in the [⛔ example claims](#-example-claims-recorded-in-lessons-not-benchmarks) table as a statistic in copy. Those are invented or unsubstantiated course-example claims, recorded so agents can recognise them. Structure only; claims not reusable.
  - **DON'T** trust a model's stated script length; count words (~150 spoken words per minute, derived). [07-02 v2 01:53](../lessons/07-02-medium-length-vsl.md)
  - **IF** two values disagree **THEN** use the "Agent default" in the conflict tables and say which source you followed.
- **See also:** [Rules](rules.md) · [Templates](templates.md) · [Libraries hub](README.md) · [VSL length bands](../concepts/vsl-length-bands.md) · [Funnel economics](../concepts/funnel-economics.md) · [Funnel selection matrix](../concepts/funnel-selection-matrix.md) · [VSL review rubric](../rubrics/vsl-review.md) · [Funnel audit](../rubrics/funnel-audit.md) · Evolve: [KPI thresholds](../../../evolve/start-here/libraries/kpi-thresholds.md)

**Labels.** "Stefan" = a rule or guideline Stefan Georgi states; "Stefan (instructor claim)" = an unverified result or stat; "Stefan (doc)" / "(screen)" = course document, slide or on-screen text; "<Guest> (guest claim)" = guest statement (12-02 Dynh Nguyen, 12-03 David O'Connell, 12-04 Max Finn, 16-01 Ethan Bence, 16-02 Eddie Maalouf, 16-04 Dylan Vanas, 16-05 Patrick Stiles); "Claude output (doc)" / "LLM output" = a model's text shown in the lesson; "swipe" = a competitor or example page; "(derived)" = arithmetic done on the lesson page, not said in the video. ⛔ = example claim, never reuse.

## Decision quick reference (derived)
The most-used numbers, condensed from the rows below. Check the linked row before relying on one.

| Decision | Number to use | Source |
|---|---|---|
| VSL length band to build | Long 25–60 min · medium 6–16 (target 8–15, max ~20) · 3–7 min · short 1–4 (mostly 1–2) | [06-01 00:30](../lessons/06-01-claude-vsl-projects-setup-overview.md) [04-08 v2 02:03](../lessons/04-08-medium-vsls-structures-and-examples.md) (C-V1) |
| Straight-sale VSL length that converts best | ~20 min best, 45 min worst; a cliff at ~1 hour (guest claim; slide 53 says 15 and 35 min) | [16-05 35:17–36:42](../lessons/16-05-vsl-benchmarks-data-and-best-practices-with-patrick-stiles.md) |
| Close share of a full VSL | ~25–35%, the longest section (04-06 page: 30–45%; Nooro ~45%) | [04-05 20:08–26:24](../lessons/04-05-vsl-product-buildup-and-reveal.md) |
| Section size sanity check | "Punch in the gut" section ~550–750 words; 1,600 is too long | [04-05 18:40–20:08](../lessons/04-05-vsl-product-buildup-and-reveal.md) |
| Script length from words | ~150 spoken words per minute (derived) | [07-02 v2 01:53](../lessons/07-02-medium-length-vsl.md) [07-03 page](../lessons/07-03-3-7-minute-vsl.md) |
| VSL conversion baseline | 1.78% all videos; ~8% (slide 8.24%) book-a-call (guest claim) | [16-05 04:14](../lessons/16-05-vsl-benchmarks-data-and-best-practices-with-patrick-stiles.md) |
| VSL engagement checkpoints | 33% at 2 min · ~28% at the CTA drop · 3% at the end (guest claim) | [16-05 19:36; slide 31](../lessons/16-05-vsl-benchmarks-data-and-best-practices-with-patrick-stiles.md) |
| Unmute | Average 40%; average unmute at ~7 s (guest claim) | [16-05 24:04–25:29](../lessons/16-05-vsl-benchmarks-data-and-best-practices-with-patrick-stiles.md) |
| Conversions after the CTA | 47% of conversions come from the 3% who reach the end (guest claim) | [16-05 30:44–31:14](../lessons/16-05-vsl-benchmarks-data-and-best-practices-with-patrick-stiles.md) |
| Native landing-page CTR target | Jump page ~40–50% · lead-gen ~30% · mid/long advertorial ~25–30% (guest claim) | [12-02 v3 13:05; 21:05; 25:27; 32:13](../lessons/12-02-native-ads.md) (C-N1) |
| Native publisher start list | ~100–150 publishers | [12-02 v3 32:43](../lessons/12-02-native-ads.md) |
| TikTok creative cadence | ≥4 new creatives a week; win rate 1 in 4 (25–33%), 1 in 8–10 at start; 75–80% net-new angles in a young account (guest claim) | [12-04 v5 03:19–04:14; v6 07:39–08:38](../lessons/12-04-tiktok-ads.md) |
| Funnel weight from the 15 factors | Sum of 1–5 scores: 46+ lean · 26–45 medium · ≤25 heavy | [13-01 08:21; 43:51](../lessons/13-01-hidden-factors-for-optimal-funnel-structure.md) |
| Advertorial → next page | Long (~1,000–2,000+ words) + product under $50 → PDP; short + over $50 → VSL | [01-02 17:52–19:27; docx](../lessons/01-02-foundational-knowledge-and-the-state-of-dtc-marketing-in-202.md) (C-A1) |
| Unit economics | Gross profit per customer = AOV − CPA − COGS; break-even CPA = AOV − COGS | [16-03 03:46–05:42; 48:30](../lessons/16-03-funnel-economics-with-spg.md) |
| Upsell video length | 6–16 min; 10–15 (even 18) for DR supplement funnels | [14-03 00:00](../lessons/14-03-claude-projects-for-upsells.md) [14-02 v4 05:03](../lessons/14-02-upsells-case-studies.md) (C-U1) |
| Upsell take rate | ~20% is good; better upsells move 20% → 40% (instructor and guest claims) | [16-03 25:03–26:52](../lessons/16-03-funnel-economics-with-spg.md) [16-05 42:44–43:04](../lessons/16-05-vsl-benchmarks-data-and-best-practices-with-patrick-stiles.md) (C-U2) |
| Abandon-cart timing and discount | Email 2 at +6 h, hardest in first 24 h; start 25–30%, end 40–50% within margin | [15-01 v2 05:26–06:37; v2 12:10–13:23](../lessons/15-01-1-1-abandon-cart-sequences.md) (C-E1) |
| Reorder timing | 7, 3, 1 days before run-out and on the day | [15-04 02:21–05:52](../lessons/15-04-1-4-winback-and-reorder-campaigns.md) |
| Winback discount | Start ~20%, rise to 40–50% | [15-04 08:58–10:32](../lessons/15-04-1-4-winback-and-reorder-campaigns.md) |
| Promo last day | 3+ emails (12 h, 6 h, 3 h left in the sample) | [15-03 03:14–05:34](../lessons/15-03-1-3-promotional-sequences.md) |
| Page test duration | ≥7 days to call a loser; ≥14 days to call a winner; 80–85% probability to win (guest claim) | [16-01 41:58–42:55](../lessons/16-01-cro-with-4080-marketing.md) |
| Price test step | 10–15% (guest claim) | [16-01 47:59](../lessons/16-01-cro-with-4080-marketing.md) |
| Webinar KPIs | Cold opt-in 30–35%; show rate 20% bad / 30% good / 40% great; pitch by 1:15; ~$1,000 per ad set before judging (guest claim) | [16-04 v2 15:45–18:22; 31:44; 37:34](../lessons/16-04-webinars-live-talk.md) |
| Mobile share of sales | "70% to 90%… in some cases 100%" (instructor claim) | [10-02 18:30](../lessons/10-02-in-depth-case-studies-dr-clean-and-calvin-tran.md) |

| 13-01 funnel doc [13-01 11:46; 43:51; 44:29](../lessons/13-01-hidden-factors-for-optimal-funnel-structure.md) | Heavy funnel: 35–45 min VSL or webinar | Medium #1: 8–15 min | Medium #2 in-feed mini-VSL: 3–10 min (speech: 1–8, "maybe ten") | Demonstrable product: 15–45 s ad | — |

| 14-02 Emma [14-02 v4 14:42](../lessons/14-02-upsells-case-studies.md) | Front-end VSL ~50 min | — | — | Video ads 5–30 min | Instructor claim |

**Agent default:** build to the project band you chose (06-01). For an on-page straight-sale VSL aim at ~15–25 min and treat 45+ min as a risk unless the niche is finance; for in-feed Meta VSLs 3–15 min; short ads 1–2 min. Always measure by word count.

| Ethan Bence (guest claim) [16-01 1:08:45](../lessons/16-01-cro-with-4080-marketing.md) | VIP $47 vs $97 | Deck: +45% conversion, −41% AOV; audio: "converted a lot better" |

| Stefan (instructor claim) [16-03 28:19–28:52](../lessons/16-03-funnel-economics-with-spg.md) | Blood-sugar price cut | Conversion doubled or tripled |

**Agent default:** test price in both directions in 10–15% steps [16-01 47:59](../lessons/16-01-cro-with-4080-marketing.md) and read CPA and profit per day, not conversion alone.

## Evolve Start Here comparison
Where Evolve Start Here's [KPI thresholds library](../../../evolve/start-here/libraries/kpi-thresholds.md) gives a different number or rule for the same decision. Evolve is a Meta media-buying course; RMBC is a funnel and copy course, so several metrics only look alike.

| Decision | RMBC II | Evolve Start Here | Agent default |
|---|---|---|---|
| Creative hit rate | 1 winner in 4 (25–33%), 1 in 8–10 at the start (Max Finn, guest claim) [12-04 v5 03:19–04:14](../lessons/12-04-tiktok-ads.md) | Example 20% hit rate vs 1%; raw UGC 15% vs 5% for images/videos (K205–K206, instructor claims) | Plan on Evolve's 5–20%; treat 25–33% as an upper bound. |
| Creative volume | ≥4 new creatives a week (TikTok); 15–20 creatives and 50–100 variations at a webinar launch [12-04 v5 03:19–04:14](../lessons/12-04-tiktok-ads.md) [16-04 v2 15:45](../lessons/16-04-webinars-live-talk.md) | 1 concept a day = 5–7 concepts = 15–20 ads a week for a small team; first batch 5–10 concepts (K200–K201) | Evolve cadence for Meta; ≥4 a week minimum on TikTok. |
| Scaling a winner | Raise budget in place and add to the scaling campaign; don't turn it off (TikTok) [12-04 v5 06:38–07:36](../lessons/12-04-tiktok-ads.md) | +20% after 48–72 h above target with ≥60% click-based purchases; 50–100% above KPI → raise by that % (K160–K164) | Evolve's +20% rule on Meta; Max Finn's rule on TikTok only. |
| Test duration | Pages: ≥7 days to kill, ≥14 to call a winner [16-01 41:58–42:55](../lessons/16-01-cro-with-4080-marketing.md); webinar ads ~$1,000 per ad set [16-04 v2 31:44](../lessons/16-04-webinars-live-talk.md) | Video ad tests ≥3 days, extend to 7 on traction; page tests 7 days, rarely >14 (K140–K141) | Aligned on pages; Evolve for ad tests. |
| "CTR" | Native landing-page click-through 25–50% by page type (Dynh Nguyen, guest claim) [12-02 v3 13:05–32:43](../lessons/12-02-native-ads.md) | Ad link CTR ≥1%, >1.5% good (K122–K123) | Different metrics; never compare a landing-page CTR with an ad CTR. |
| CPM | Native: chase "high CPMs with high CTRs and low CPCs" [12-02 v2 09:02](../lessons/12-02-native-ads.md); whitelisted ad ~$21 ($20.69) (Eddie Maalouf, guest claim) [16-02 19:33](../lessons/16-02-ecom-and-amazon-with-eddie-maalouf.md) | ~$10 "very, very good"; $30–$70 "really hard to manage" (K126) | Evolve bands for Meta prospecting; native CPMs are judged with CTR and CPC. |
| Engagement metrics | VSL engagement 33% at 2 min, ~28% at CTA, 3% at end; unmute 40% (Patrick Stiles, guest claim) [16-05 19:36; 24:30](../lessons/16-05-vsl-benchmarks-data-and-best-practices-with-patrick-stiles.md) | Hook ≥25%, hold ≥30% of hook; "soft metrics", decide on ROAS and CPA (K120–K125) | Player metrics locate a VSL leak; decisions use CPA, ROAS or EPS in both courses. [16-05 14:33–16:27](../lessons/16-05-vsl-benchmarks-data-and-best-practices-with-patrick-stiles.md) |
| Break-even | Break-even CPA = AOV − COGS; gross profit per customer = AOV − CPA − COGS [16-03 03:46–05:42; 48:30](../lessons/16-03-funnel-economics-with-spg.md) | Break-even ROAS = 1 ÷ gross margin; scaling ROAS = break-even + 1 (K061–K062) | Same math (derived: break-even ROAS = AOV ÷ (AOV − COGS)). Use Evolve's scaling ROAS for Meta budget moves. |
| Price and AOV floors | $45 one-time product → raise AOV inside the advertorial [09-01 18:33](../lessons/09-01-advertorials-overview-and-examples.md); cheap = under $50 → PDP [01-02 docx](../lessons/01-02-foundational-knowledge-and-the-state-of-dtc-marketing-in-202.md); micro-lander <$9 [13-01 16:12](../lessons/13-01-hidden-factors-for-optimal-funnel-structure.md); Dr. Clean COGS ~$1.50 on ~$20 (instructor claim) [11-01 02:00–03:33](../lessons/11-01-checkouts-walkthrough-part-1.md) | AOV $60+ after bundles; price ≥3x COGS + shipping, 5x good; beginners $40–$80 (K071–K074) | Use Evolve's floors to judge paid-social viability; RMBC's thresholds pick the page type. |
| Going negative on day 0 | "Totally fine model if you know what you're doing" with rebills; Stefan would "rather not" [14-01 v1 10:43–11:14](../lessons/14-01-upsells-overview.md) [16-03 49:29–51:28](../lessons/16-03-funnel-economics-with-spg.md) | Skip LTV/NCPA modelling under ~$50k/month; use blended and in-platform ROAS (K048) | Under ~$50k/month spend: don't buy customers at a day-0 loss. |
| Meta structure | Test every campaign type and placement, then cut (Eddie Maalouf, guest claim) [16-02 20:22–21:22](../lessons/16-02-ecom-and-amazon-with-eddie-maalouf.md) | One broad CBO, 3:2:2 ad sets; support campaigns only above spend gates (K001–K021) | Evolve 3:2:2. |

## Coverage and gaps
- **Sources:** all 58 lesson pages (notes, examples, tables and how-to-apply sections). Prompt blocks were excluded unless the lesson used a number in a prompt as a rule (e.g. discount ladders in the email prompt).
- **Row counts:** 1,020 rows (B001–B1020) across 24 topic tables, from 251 + 240 + 230 + 299 extracted rows (modules 1–5, 6–12, 13–15, 16); 72 of them are in the ⛔ table, kept only so agents can recognise and refuse those claims.
- **Evidence limits:** no lesson shows a controlled test with raw data. 16-05 figures are Vidalytics platform aggregates ("these aren't split tests"); 16-01 to 16-04 figures come from guests' decks and clients; Stefan's figures are anecdotes or worked hypotheticals. Several lesson pages contain arithmetic slips, listed in [C-X](#c-x-arithmetic-and-transcription-slips-found-on-lesson-pages).
- **Labelling choices:** 12-03 and 12-04 lesson pages tag guest figures "instructor claim"; here they are "(guest claim)" with the guest's name. Copy Accelerator PDF figures in 14-02 are not Stefan's and are labelled as such.
